# Agents · A · part 39

971 entries, audience-reviewer to audit-scout. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **audience-reviewer**: Fresh critical review of one product-documentation page before it may publish — judges it against the code it claims to describe and against a PM reader who will never read that code. Dispatched by the handbook-docs audience gate; never edits files. ([ypngslo/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ypngslo/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/handbook/agents/audience-reviewer.md))
- **audience-scout**: Finds the real, named places online where one product's audience gathers — subreddits, Discord servers, forums, newsletters, creators — each verified by a page actually fetched. Use when a marketing skill needs audience research, or when asked "where does &lt;audience&gt; hang out online". ([stylusnexus/agent-plugins](https://github.com/stylusnexus/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/go-to-market/agents/audience-scout.md) · ★1)
- **audience-segmentation**: Analyzes visitor cohorts, geographic distribution, device/platform mix, and new-vs-returning patterns to identify best audience segments and churn risk. Use when profiling who visits your sites or spotting engagement decline in key cohorts. Trigger with "analyze my audience", "who are my best visitors". ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/analytics/web-analytics/agents/audience-segmentation.md) · ★2.9k)
- **audience-segmentation-agent**: MUST BE USED to analyze customer data and create advanced audience segments using behavioral, psychographic, and value-based methodologies for hyper-personalized marketing. ([whodaniel/The-New-Fuse](https://github.com/whodaniel/The-New-Fuse/blob/HEAD/.agent/fleet/users/agents/audience-segmentation-agent.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **audience-simulator**: &gt; **Роль:** Имитация реакции целевой аудитории на контент ([Peroksa/content-factory](https://github.com/Peroksa/content-factory/blob/HEAD/claudecode-content-factory/projects/_template/agents/audience-simulator.md) · ★1)
- **audience-skeptic**: Reviews forgectl as the Skeptic adoption archetype — building the strongest honest case against adoption. Use when a user-facing surface (CLI, README, skill, docs, feature) changes, or as a seat in an audience-review panel. Read-only. ([cameronsjo/forgectl](https://github.com/cameronsjo/forgectl/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/audience-skeptic.md) · ★1)
- **audience-sociologist**: Judges a Plate & Price short or video as a viewer from the target audience would live it (social, cultural and emotional lens). Use it when reviewing a cut, caption set or cover before a video is final. ([panurjoma/Mecha](https://github.com/panurjoma/Mecha/blob/HEAD/plate-and-price/.claude/agents/audience-sociologist.md))
- **audience-strategist**: Use when the decision depends on buying situations, customer jobs, triggers, anxieties, identity, communities, or observed customer language. ([imMamdouhaboammar/marketing-council-pack](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/marketing-council-pack/blob/HEAD/agents/audience-strategist.md) · ★7)
- **audience-tracker**: Creator-pack audience growth specialist. Invoke for cross-channel growth tracking, engagement-rate audits, lead-magnet conversion analysis, and email-list health checks. Reads archetype-packs/creator/audience/, content/lead-magnets.md, content/posts/published/. Drafts to drafts/. ([Bimbi-Dev/bimbOS](https://github.com/Bimbi-Dev/bimbOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/audience-tracker.md) · ★1)
- **audience-visionary**: Reviews forgectl as the Visionary adoption archetype — judging trajectory and strategic ceiling over current state. ([cameronsjo/forgectl](https://github.com/cameronsjo/forgectl/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/audience-visionary.md) · ★1)
- **audience-writer**: Use this agent (OPTIONAL, Phase 5, after the chain is published) to write nanopubs/audience.json — the plain-language, per-audience retellings that scripts/build_story.py turns into the story page's "For citizens" / "For schools" tabs. Runs ONCE at build time; the reader pays nothing and the deterministic "Record" tab never depends on it. - **audio-iclora-roadmap**: Experiment-sequencing + order verifier for the audio→video IC-LoRA effort. Use to decide WHAT to run next and to GATE-KEEP the order: confirm the prior step's gate is green, the next step isolates exactly one new variable, a falsifiable prediction + objective gate are written down BEFORE running, and no cheaper falsifying test is being skipped.
